Online shopping has many advantages. First, it is convenient. We can shop at any time and from any place. We don't need to leave home or wait in __2__. Second, prices are often lower, because online sellers have fewer __3__, such as rent. Third, we can compare prices and read reviews easily, so we can make a better choice. Fourth, online shops offer more products. We can buy things that are not __4__ in local stores.

However, online shopping also has some disadvantages. First, we cannot see or touch the real product before buying. What we see on the screen may be different from what we get. Second, there may be problems with delivery. We may wait a long time, or the goods may be __5__ on the way. Third, personal information may be __6__ if we use unsafe websites. Fourth, returning goods can be troublesome.

So how can we shop online safely? First, buy from reliable shops and websites. Check the reviews and the seller's __7__. Second, read the product description carefully before you pay. Third, protect your personal information. Don't use public Wi-Fi when you pay. Fourth, keep the receipt and know the return policy.

In a word, online shopping is a coin with two sides. It brings us convenience but also risks. If we are __8__ enough, we can enjoy the benefits and avoid the problems. As technology develops, online shopping will become even more __9__ in the future. It is changing not only the way we shop but also the way we __10__.

**参考译文：** 近年来，网上购物变得越来越流行。许多人现在不去商店，而是用手机或电脑点几下就买到东西。

网上购物有许多好处。首先，它很方便。我们可以在任何时间、任何地点购物，不必出门，也不用排队。其次，价格往往更低，因为网店的开销更少，比如租金。第三，我们可以轻松比较价格、查看评论，从而做出更好的选择。第四，网店提供更多商品，我们可以买到当地商店没有的东西。

然而，网上购物也有一些缺点。首先，购买前我们看不到、摸不到真正的商品，屏幕上看到的可能和拿到的不一样。其次，送货可能出问题，我们可能等很久，或者货物在途中损坏。第三，如果我们使用不安全的网站，个人信息可能泄露。第四，退货可能很麻烦。

那么，我们怎样才能安全地网上购物呢？首先，从可靠的商店和网站购买，查看评论和卖家的信誉。其次，付款前仔细阅读商品描述。第三，保护个人信息，付款时不要使用公共无线网络。第四，保留收据，了解退货政策。

总之，网上购物是一枚有两面的硬币。它带给我们便利，也带来风险。如果我们足够谨慎，就能享受好处、避免问题。随着技术发展，网上购物在未来会变得更加普遍。它不仅改变着我们购物的方式，也改变着我们生活的方式。

**1.** A. clicks　B. hours　C. days　D. people

**2.** A. money　B. line　C. trouble　D. danger

**3.** A. friends　B. costs　C. products　D. workers

**4.** A. available　B. cheap　C. good　D. popular

**5.** A. sold　B. bought　C. damaged　D. made

**6.** A. safe　B. kept　C. saved　D. stolen

**7.** A. money　B. reputation　C. age　D. height

**8.** A. careless　B. rich　C. careful　D. lazy

**9.** A. expensive　B. rare　C. dangerous　D. common

**10.** A. live　B. work　C. study　D. play

**参考答案：** 1. A　2. B　3. B　4. A　5. C　6. D　7. B　8. C　9. D　10. A

**解析：**
- **1. A** clicks：现在人们只需在手机或电脑上点几下（几次点击）就能购物。B hours、C days 与"便利"矛盾，D people 不合。
- **2. B** line：网上购物不必出门、不用"排队"，`wait in line` 固定搭配。A money、C trouble、D danger 均不能与 wait in 构成此义。
- **3. B** costs：网店成本更低，比如"租金"等费用更少，所以价格更低。A friends、C products、D workers 均不能与 rent 并列。
- **4. A** available：网上能买到本地商店"买不到"的东西。B cheap、C good、D popular 均不能与 in local stores 构成"买得到"。
- **5. C** damaged：快递途中货物可能被"损坏"。A sold、B bought 与"运输问题"无关，D made 不合。
- **6. D** stolen：使用不安全的网站，个人信息可能被"盗取"。A safe 相反，B kept、C saved 无风险义。
- **7. B** reputation：购物前查看评价和卖家的"信誉"。A money、C age、D height 均与"可信度"无关。
- **8. C** careful：只要足够"谨慎"，就能享受好处、避开问题。A careless 相反，B rich、D lazy 不合。
- **9. D** common：随着技术发展，网上购物会更加"普遍"，与"改变生活方式"呼应。A expensive、B rare、C dangerous 均与"未来趋势"矛盾。
- **10. A** live：它不只改变我们购物的方式，也改变我们"生活"的方式。B work、C study、D play 都太片面。

**重点词汇：** online shopping 网上购物；convenient 方便的；rent 租金；review 评价；delivery 快递；reliable 可靠的；receipt 收据；return policy 退货政策。

## 完形填空 53 · 商务职场（A Job Interview）

**Directions:** 阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Finding a good job is important for everyone. But before you get a job, you usually need to pass a job interview. A good interview can help you get the job you __1__.

How can you do well in a job interview? Here are some __2__.

First, prepare well before the interview. Learn about the company — what it does, how big it is and what its culture is like. Think about the questions the interviewer may __3__, such as "Why do you want this job?" and "What are your strong points?" Prepare your answers in advance.

Second, dress properly. You should look clean and tidy. Wearing suitable clothes shows that you __4__ the interviewer and take the job seriously. It also helps you make a good first __5__.

Third, be on time. Arriving late will give a bad impression. It is better to arrive ten or fifteen minutes __6__. If you are late, you should call and explain the reason.

Fourth, be confident but not too proud. When you answer questions, look at the interviewer and speak clearly. Don't be too nervous. If you don't understand a question, you can ask him to __7__ it.

Fifth, show your strengths. Tell the interviewer what you are good at and what you can do for the company. Give examples to support your words.

Sixth, be honest. Never tell lies about your education or experience. If the interviewer finds out, you will lose the __8__. Remember, honesty is the best policy.

After the interview, you can send a short e-mail to __9__ the interviewer. This small action may leave a good impression.

In a word, a successful interview needs good preparation, a proper attitude and honest answers. If you follow these tips, you will have a better __10__ of getting the job.

**参考译文：** 找到一份好工作对每个人都很重要。但在得到工作之前，你通常需要通过求职面试。一次好的面试能帮你得到你想要的工作。

怎样才能在求职面试中表现出色呢？这里有一些建议。

首先，面试前要做好准备。了解这家公司——它做什么、规模多大、文化如何。想一想面试官可能问的问题，比如你为什么想要这份工作，以及你有什么优点。提前准备好答案。

其次，着装得体。你应该看起来干净整洁。穿着合适的衣服表明你尊重面试官、认真对待这份工作，也有助于你留下良好的第一印象。

第三，准时。迟到会给人不好的印象。最好提前十到十五分钟到达。如果迟到了，你应该打电话说明原因。

第四，自信但不要骄傲。回答问题时，看着面试官，清楚地表达。不要紧张。如果没听懂某个问题，你可以请他解释一下。

第五，展示你的长处。告诉面试官你擅长什么、能为公司做什么。用例子来支持你的话。

第六，诚实。绝不要在学历或经历上撒谎。如果面试官发现，你就会失去这个机会。记住，诚实是上策。

面试结束后，你可以发一封简短的邮件感谢面试官。这个小小的举动可能留下好印象。

总之，一次成功的面试需要充分的准备、恰当的态度和诚实的回答。如果你遵循这些建议，你得到工作的机会就会更大。

**1.** A. hate　B. lose　C. leave　D. want

**2.** A. problems　B. tips　C. rules　D. laws

**3.** A. ask　B. answer　C. forget　D. hide

**4.** A. hate　B. ignore　C. respect　D. avoid

**5.** A. mistake　B. impression　C. trouble　D. noise

**6.** A. late　B. early　C. again　D. away

**7.** A. forget　B. stop　C. repeat　D. leave

**8.** A. money　B. friends　C. chance　D. job

**9.** A. blame　B. avoid　C. ignore　D. thank

**10.** A. problem　B. trouble　C. danger　D. opportunity

**参考答案：** 1. D　2. B　3. A　4. C　5. B　6. B　7. C　8. C　9. D　10. D

**解析：**
- **1. D** want：好的面试能帮你得到你"想要"的工作。A hate、B lose、C leave 均与"求职"目的相反。
- **2. B** tips：下文列出的是一系列"建议"，`Here are some tips.` 与后文 First/Second... 结构呼应。A problems、C rules、D laws 均不合。
- **3. A** ask：想一想面试官可能"问"的问题，与后面两个示例问题呼应。B answer、C forget、D hide 均与"示例问题"矛盾。
- **4. C** respect：得体的着装表明你"尊重"面试官、重视这份工作。A hate、B ignore、D avoid 均相反。
- **5. B** impression：着装得体有助于留下良好的第一"印象"，`make a good impression` 固定搭配。A mistake、C trouble、D noise 均不符。
- **6. B** early：最好提前十到十五分钟"到"，与"迟到会留下坏印象"相对。A late 相反，C again、D away 不合。
- **7. C** repeat：没听清问题时，可以请他"重复"一遍。A forget、B stop、D leave 均不合。
- **8. C** chance：如果被面试官发现撒谎，你就会失去这个"机会"。A money、B friends 与"求职"无关，D job 与句意重复且不如 chance 贴切。
- **9. D** thank：面试后可以发一封短信"感谢"面试官，与"留下好印象"呼应。A blame、B avoid、C ignore 均相反。
- **10. D** opportunity：遵循这些建议，你就更有"机会"拿到这份工作。A problem、B trouble、C danger 均为负面。

**重点词汇：** job interview 求职面试；interviewer 面试官；in advance 提前；tidy 整洁的；strength 优势；education 学历；opportunity 机会。

## 完形填空 54 · 商务职场（How to Deal with Stress）

**Directions:** 阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Stress is a normal part of life. Everyone feels stressed sometimes, especially when we face exams, work or personal problems. A little stress can __1__ us to work harder, but too much stress is bad for our health.

What causes stress? There are many reasons. For students, exams and too much homework are common __2__. For workers, a heavy workload, long hours and a difficult boss may bring stress. Other causes include money problems, health problems and relationship problems.

Too much stress can harm us in many ways. It may make us feel tired, worried and unhappy. It can also cause health problems, such as headaches, stomachaches and sleeping problems. In serious cases, stress may even __3__ to depression.

So how can we deal with stress? Here are some useful __4__.

First, find out the cause. When we know what makes us stressed, we can deal with it better. For example, if exams make us __5__, we can prepare earlier and study in a planned way.

Second, take exercise. Exercise is a good way to __6__ stress. When we run, swim or play ball games, our body produces chemicals that make us feel good. After exercise, we often feel relaxed and happy.

Third, talk to others. Don't keep your worries to yourself. Talking to a friend, a family member or a teacher can help you feel better. They may give you useful __7__ or simply listen to you.

Fourth, have a good rest. When we are tired, we get stressed more easily. So make sure you get enough sleep and eat __8__ food.

Fifth, do something you enjoy. Listening to music, reading a book or taking a walk can help you forget your worries for a while. Sixth, think positively. Try to see the __9__ side of things. Instead of worrying about what may go wrong, think about what you can do to make things better.

In a word, stress is not always bad, but we should learn to control it. If we keep a positive attitude and take good care of ourselves, we can live a healthier and happier __10__.

**参考译文：** 压力是生活中正常的一部分。每个人有时都会感到有压力，尤其是在面对考试、工作或个人问题时。一点点压力能促使我们更努力，但压力过大对健康有害。

压力是由什么引起的呢？原因有很多。对学生来说，考试和过多的作业是常见原因；对上班族来说，繁重的工作量、长时间的加班和难相处的老板都可能带来压力。其他原因还包括金钱问题、健康问题和人际关系问题。

压力过大可能以多种方式伤害我们。它可能让我们感到疲惫、焦虑和不快乐，还可能导致健康问题，如头痛、胃痛和睡眠问题。严重时，压力甚至可能导致抑郁。

那么，我们该如何应对压力呢？这里有一些有用的建议。

首先，找出原因。当我们知道自己为什么有压力时，就能更好地应对它。例如，如果考试让我们紧张，我们可以提前准备，有计划地复习。

其次，进行锻炼。锻炼是缓解压力的好方法。当我们跑步、游泳或打球时，身体会产生让人感觉良好的化学物质。锻炼之后，我们常常感到放松和愉快。

第三，与他人交谈。不要把烦恼憋在心里。和朋友、家人或老师聊聊能让你感觉好一些。他们可能会给你有用的建议，或者只是倾听你。

第四，好好休息。当我们疲惫时，更容易感到有压力。所以要保证充足的睡眠，吃健康的食物。

第五，做些自己喜欢的事。听音乐、读书或散步都能帮你暂时忘掉烦恼。第六，积极思考。试着看到事物好的一面。与其担心可能出错的地方，不如想想你能做些什么让事情变得更好。

总之，压力并不总是坏事，但我们应该学会控制它。如果我们保持积极的心态，好好照顾自己，就能过上更健康、更快乐的生活。

**1.** A. stop　B. force　C. allow　D. push

**2.** A. answers　B. causes　C. results　D. reasons

**3.** A. come　B. add　C. look　D. lead

**4.** A. problems　B. ways　C. troubles　D. dangers

**5.** A. happy　B. relaxed　C. nervous　D. excited

**6.** A. increase　B. cause　C. bring　D. reduce

**7.** A. money　B. advice　C. food　D. clothes

**8.** A. fast　B. junk　C. little　D. healthy

**9.** A. bright　B. dark　C. sad　D. worse

**10.** A. life　B. money　C. job　D. day

**参考答案：** 1. D　2. B　3. D　4. B　5. C　6. D　7. B　8. D　9. A　10. A

**解析：**
- **1. D** push：一点点压力能"推动"我们更努力，与"压力过大有害"形成对比。A stop 相反，B force 语气过强，C allow 不合。
- **2. B** causes：对学生而言，考试和过多的作业是常见的"压力来源"。A answers、C results、D reasons 都不能与"压力原因"精准对应（causes of stress 为固定说法）。
- **3. D** lead：严重时压力甚至会"导致"抑郁，`lead to` 固定搭配。A come to、B add to、C look to 均无"导致"义。
- **4. B** ways：下文列举的是应对压力的几种"方法"。A problems、C troubles、D dangers 均为负面，与"解决办法"矛盾。
- **5. C** nervous：考试让我们"紧张"，与"提前准备、有计划地复习"呼应。A happy、B relaxed、D excited 与"备考压力"不符。
- **6. D** reduce：运动是"减轻"压力的好方法，与"感觉放松快乐"呼应。A increase、B cause、C bring 均相反。
- **7. B** advice：朋友、家人或老师可能给你有用的"建议"，`give sb. advice` 固定搭配。A money、C food、D clothes 均不合。
- **8. D** healthy：要保证充足睡眠、吃"健康的"食物，与"更好地应对压力"呼应。A fast、B junk、C little 均为负面。
- **9. A** bright：要看到事情"好的一面"，`the bright side` 固定表达，与 think positively 呼应。B dark、C sad、D worse 均与"积极"相反。
- **10. A** life：保持积极态度、照顾好自己，就能过上更健康幸福的"生活"。B money、C job、D day 均不搭配 live a happier。

**重点词汇：** stress 压力；workload 工作量；depression 抑郁；deal with 应对；relaxed 放松的；advice 建议；positively 积极地；bright side 好的一面。

## 完形填空 55 · 健康生活（Healthy Eating Habits）

**Directions:** 阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Good eating habits are important for our health. What we eat and how we eat can __1__ how we feel every day.

First, we should eat a __2__ diet. Our body needs different kinds of food. We need rice, bread and noodles for energy. We need meat, fish, eggs and beans for protein. We also need plenty of vegetables and fruit for vitamins. A balanced diet means eating the right __3__ of each kind of food.

Second, we should eat regular meals. Skipping breakfast is a bad habit. Many students go to school without breakfast, and then they feel tired and cannot __4__ in class. Eating three meals a day at regular times helps keep our body working well.

Third, we should not eat too much. Eating too much can make us fat, and being __5__ may cause many health problems, such as heart disease and high blood pressure. So we should control the amount of food we eat. It is better to eat until we are almost full than to eat until we can't __6__.

Fourth, we should choose healthy snacks. Instead of eating sweets, chips and fried food, we can eat some fruit, nuts or yogurt. These snacks are both tasty and __7__.

Fifth, we should drink enough water. Water is __8__ for our body. It is much better than sweet drinks, which contain a lot of __9__.

In a word, healthy eating is not difficult. It just needs a little care. If we develop good eating habits, we can stay healthy and feel __10__.

**参考译文：** 良好的饮食习惯对我们的健康很重要。我们吃什么、怎么吃，能影响我们每天的感觉。

首先，我们应该均衡饮食。我们的身体需要不同种类的食物。我们需要米饭、面包和面条来提供能量；需要肉、鱼、蛋和豆类来提供蛋白质；还需要大量蔬菜和水果来提供维生素。均衡饮食意味着每种食物都吃得适量。

其次，我们应该规律进餐。不吃早餐是个坏习惯。许多学生不吃早餐就去上学，然后感到疲惫，上课无法集中注意力。一日三餐按时吃，有助于身体正常运转。

第三，我们不应吃得太多。吃太多会让我们发胖，而肥胖可能引起许多健康问题，如心脏病和高血压。所以我们应该控制食量。吃到差不多饱比吃到撑不下要好。

第四，我们应该选择健康的零食。与其吃糖、薯片和油炸食品，不如吃些水果、坚果或酸奶。这些零食既美味又健康。

第五，我们应该喝足够的水。水对我们的身体至关重要。它比含糖量很高的甜饮料好得多。

总之，健康饮食并不难，只需要一点用心。如果我们养成良好的饮食习惯，就能保持健康、感觉良好。

**1.** A. affect　B. forget　C. hide　D. stop

**2.** A. balanced　B. fast　C. junk　D. simple

**3.** A. number　B. price　C. amount　D. color

**4.** A. sleep　B. play　C. concentrate　D. talk

**5.** A. thin　B. tall　C. weak　D. overweight

**6.** A. sleep　B. move　C. eat　D. drink

**7.** A. cheap　B. healthy　C. sweet　D. salty

**8.** A. bad　B. harmful　C. necessary　D. useless

**9.** A. water　B. sugar　C. milk　D. juice

**10.** A. tired　B. sad　C. worried　D. energetic

**参考答案：** 1. A　2. A　3. C　4. C　5. D　6. B　7. B　8. C　9. B　10. D

**解析：**
- **1. A** affect：我们吃什么、怎么吃会"影响"每天的状态，`affect` 意为"影响"。B forget、C hide、D stop 均不合逻辑。
- **2. A** balanced：应吃"均衡的"饮食，段落末尾"balanced diet"一句直接印证。B fast、C junk、D simple 均与营养均衡无关。
- **3. C** amount：均衡饮食指每种食物吃合适的"数量"，`the right amount of` 固定搭配。A number 用于可数名词，B price、D color 均不符。
- **4. C** concentrate：不吃早餐会使学生上课难以"集中注意力"，`concentrate in class` 符合。A sleep、B play、D talk 与"上课"语境不符。
- **5. D** overweight：吃太多会变胖，"超重"会引发多种疾病，与 heart disease、high blood pressure 呼应。A thin 相反，B tall、C weak 与"吃太多"无因果。
- **6. B** move：`eat until we can't move` 意为"撑得动不了"，与"吃到差不多饱即可"形成对比。A sleep、C eat、D drink 均不合此表达。
- **7. B** healthy：水果、坚果等零食既美味又"健康"。A cheap 不是营养角度，C sweet、D salty 与"健康零食"矛盾。
- **8. C** necessary：水是身体"必需的"，与"比甜饮料好得多"呼应。A bad、B harmful、D useless 均为负面。
- **9. B** sugar：甜饮料含大量"糖"，因此不如白水健康。A water 与 sweet drinks 矛盾，C milk、D juice 不是甜的代名词。
- **10. D** energetic：养成好的饮食习惯，就能保持健康、感到"精力充沛"。A tired、B sad、C worried 均为负面。

**重点词汇：** balanced diet 均衡饮食；energy 能量；protein 蛋白质；vitamin 维生素；skip breakfast 不吃早餐；concentrate 集中注意力；overweight 超重的；blood pressure 血压。

## 完形填空 56 · 自然科学（How Plants Grow）

**Directions:** 阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Plants are all around us. They provide us with food, oxygen and many other things. But how do plants __1__?

Most plants begin their lives as seeds. A seed needs three things to grow: water, warmth and air. When a seed gets these, it begins to __2__. A small root grows down into the soil, and a small shoot grows up toward the light.

The root takes in water and minerals from the soil. The shoot grows into a stem with leaves. The leaves are like small factories. Using sunlight, water and carbon dioxide, they make food for the plant. This process is called __3__. It also gives out oxygen, which all animals and people need to __4__.

As the plant grows, it becomes taller and stronger. Some plants grow very fast. Bamboo, for example, can grow almost one meter a day in good __5__. Other plants, such as trees, grow slowly and may live for hundreds of years.

Plants need care to grow well. They need enough water, but too much water may __6__ them. They need sunlight, but some plants cannot stand strong sunlight. They also need good soil. Farmers add fertilizer to help their crops grow __7__.

Plants are important to us. We eat them, wear clothes __8__ from them, and build houses with wood. They also help clean the air and protect the soil. Without plants, life on the earth would not be __9__. So let's take good care of plants and the environment. A green world is a __10__ world.

**参考译文：** 植物在我们周围随处可见。它们为我们提供食物、氧气和许多其他东西。但植物是怎样生长的呢？

大多数植物以种子开始生命。一粒种子需要三样东西才能生长：水、温暖和空气。当种子得到这些，它就开始发芽。一条小根向下扎进土壤，一棵小芽向上朝着光亮生长。

根从土壤中吸取水分和矿物质。芽长成带叶子的茎。叶子就像小工厂。它们利用阳光、水和二氧化碳为植物制造养分。这个过程叫做光合作用。它还释放出氧气，所有动物和人都需要呼吸氧气。

随着植物生长，它变得更高更强壮。有些植物长得非常快。例如，竹子在良好的条件下一天能长近一米。另一些植物，如树木，生长缓慢，可能活上几百年。

植物需要照料才能长得好。它们需要足够的水，但过多的水可能会伤害它们。它们需要阳光，但有些植物受不了强烈的阳光。它们还需要良好的土壤。农民施肥来帮助庄稼长得更好。

植物对我们很重要。我们吃它们，穿用它们制成的衣服，用木材盖房子。它们还有助于净化空气、保护土壤。没有植物，地球上的生命将不复存在。所以让我们好好照料植物和环境。绿色的世界才是美丽的世界。

**1.** A. grow　B. sleep　C. move　D. run

**2.** A. stop　B. sprout　C. die　D. sleep

**3.** A. breathing　B. running　C. photosynthesis　D. sleeping

**4.** A. breathe　B. eat　C. sleep　D. run

**5.** A. money　B. friends　C. trouble　D. conditions

**6.** A. help　B. kill　C. water　D. feed

**7.** A. worse　B. slower　C. better　D. less

**8.** A. grown　B. sold　C. eaten　D. made

**9.** A. possible　B. impossible　C. difficult　D. dangerous

**10.** A. sad　B. boring　C. healthy　D. dark

**参考答案：** 1. A　2. B　3. C　4. A　5. D　6. B　7. C　8. D　9. A　10. C

**解析：**
- **1. A** grow：全文讲的是植物如何"生长"，与标题和下文 seed、root 等呼应。B sleep、C move、D run 均不合。
- **2. B** sprout：种子获得水、温度和空气后开始"发芽"，`sprout` 意为"发芽"。A stop、C die、D sleep 均与生长过程相反。
- **3. C** photosynthesis：叶子利用阳光、水和二氧化碳制造养分的过程叫"光合作用"。A breathing、B running、D sleeping 均与植物制造养分无关。
- **4. A** breathe：光合作用释放出所有动物和人都需要"呼吸"的氧气。B eat、C sleep、D run 均不能与 oxygen 直接搭配。
- **5. D** conditions：`in good conditions` 意为"在良好的条件下"，指竹子一天长近一米。A money、B friends、C trouble 均不合。
- **6. B** kill：水太多可能"淹死/害死"植物，与前句"需要足够的水"形成对比。A help、C water、D feed 均与转折关系矛盾。
- **7. C** better：农民施肥是为了让庄稼长得"更好"，`grow better` 符合。A worse、D less 相反，B slower 不合。
- **8. D** made：我们穿用植物"制成"的衣服，`clothes made from them` 意为"由它们制成的衣服"。A grown、B sold、C eaten 均不能表示"制成"。
- **9. A** possible：没有植物，地球上的生命就"不可能"存在。B impossible 与 would not be 构成双重否定而变义，C difficult、D dangerous 均不合。
- **10. C** healthy：绿色的世界是"健康的"世界，与环保主题呼应。A sad、B boring、D dark 均为负面。

**重点词汇：** oxygen 氧气；seed 种子；sprout 发芽；root 根；stem 茎；carbon dioxide 二氧化碳；photosynthesis 光合作用；fertilizer 肥料。

## 完形填空 57 · 环境保护（Saving Energy in Daily Life）

**Directions:** 阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Energy is important in our daily life. We use it to light our homes, cook our meals and drive our cars. However, energy is not __1__. Most of the energy we use comes from coal, oil and gas, which will run out one day. Burning them also __2__ the environment. So it is important to save energy.

How can we save energy in daily life? Here are some simple __3__.

First, save electricity. Turn off the lights and other electrical appliances when you leave a room. Don't leave the TV or computer on when nobody is watching. Use energy-saving bulbs instead of __4__ ones. These bulbs use less electricity and last longer.

Second, save water. Although water covers most of the earth, fresh water is __5__. Fix leaking taps, take shorter showers, and reuse water when possible. For example, we can use the water from washing rice to __6__ the plants.

Third, use public transport. Cars use a lot of fuel and produce a lot of waste gas. If we take the bus, the subway or ride a bike, we can save energy and reduce __7__. If the distance is short, walking is the best choice.

Fourth, reduce, reuse and recycle. Buying fewer things means using less energy in production. Reusing things, such as plastic bags and bottles, can also save __8__. And recycling paper, glass and metal can turn waste into useful materials.

Fifth, eat local and seasonal food. Food that is grown nearby needs less fuel to be __9__ to us. Eating seasonal food is also cheaper and fresher.

In a word, saving energy is saving our future. Everyone can do something, no matter how small. If we all take action, we can make a big __10__.

**参考译文：** 能源在我们的日常生活中很重要。我们用它来给家里照明、做饭、开车。然而，能源并不是取之不尽的。我们使用的大部分能源来自煤、石油和天然气，它们总有一天会耗尽。燃烧它们还会污染环境。所以节约能源很重要。

我们怎样才能在日常生活中节约能源呢？这里有一些简单的建议。

首先，节约用电。离开房间时关掉电灯和其他电器。没人看的时候不要让电视或电脑开着。使用节能灯泡，而不是普通的灯泡。这些灯泡用电更少，寿命更长。

其次，节约用水。虽然水覆盖了地球的大部分，但淡水是有限的。修好漏水的水龙头，缩短淋浴时间，尽可能重复用水。例如，我们可以用洗米的水来浇植物。

第三，使用公共交通。汽车消耗大量燃料，产生大量废气。如果我们坐公交车、地铁或骑自行车，就能节约能源、减少污染。如果距离短，步行是最好的选择。

第四，减少使用、重复使用和回收利用。少买东西意味着在生产中消耗更少的能源。重复使用塑料袋和瓶子等物品，也能节约能源。而回收纸张、玻璃和金属，可以把废物变成有用的材料。

第五，吃本地和当季的食物。在附近种植的食物运到我们手中需要更少的燃料。吃当季的食物也更便宜、更新鲜。

总之，节约能源就是拯救我们的未来。每个人都能做些事，无论多么微小。如果我们都行动起来，就能带来巨大的改变。

**1.** A. endless　B. useful　C. cheap　D. clean

**2.** A. protects　B. pollutes　C. cleans　D. saves

**3.** A. problems　B. rules　C. ways　D. laws

**4.** A. ordinary　B. new　C. modern　D. special

**5.** A. enough　B. clean　C. sweet　D. limited

**6.** A. pick　B. water　C. cut　D. sell

**7.** A. money　B. friends　C. pollution　D. time

**8.** A. money　B. time　C. friends　D. resources

**9.** A. sold　B. transported　C. eaten　D. made

**10.** A. mistake　B. trouble　C. difference　D. problem

**参考答案：** 1. A　2. B　3. C　4. A　5. D　6. B　7. C　8. D　9. B　10. C

**解析：**
- **1. A** endless：能源并非"取之不尽"，与"煤、石油、天然气终会枯竭"呼应。B useful、C cheap、D clean 均与"会用完"无关。
- **2. B** pollutes：燃烧化石燃料还会"污染"环境，与"要节约能源"呼应。A protects、C cleans、D saves 均相反。
- **3. C** ways：下文列举的是节约能源的几种"方法"，与 How can we 呼应。A problems、B rules、D laws 均不合。
- **4. A** ordinary：用节能灯泡代替"普通的"灯泡，与"更省电、更耐用"呼应。B new、C modern 与 energy-saving 不对立，D special 不合。
- **5. D** limited：虽然水覆盖地球大部分，但淡水是"有限的"，与节约用水呼应。A enough 相反，B clean、C sweet 均不说明"要节约"的原因。
- **6. B** water：淘米水可以用来"浇"花，`water the plants` 中 water 作动词。A pick、C cut、D sell 均与"再利用水"无关。
- **7. C** pollution：乘坐公共交通能节约能源、减少"污染"，与"汽车排放废气"呼应。A money、B friends、D time 均不能与 reduce 构成此义。
- **8. D** resources：重复使用塑料袋、瓶子能节约"资源"。A money、B time 不全面，C friends 无关。
- **9. B** transported：附近种植的食物运输到我们身边需要的燃料更少，`be transported to` 意为"被运送到"。A sold、C eaten、D made 均与"减少燃料"无关。
- **10. C** difference：如果人人都行动起来，就能产生很大的"影响"，`make a difference` 固定搭配。A mistake、B trouble、D problem 均为负面。

**重点词汇：** energy 能源；run out 用完；coal 煤；appliance 电器；energy-saving 节能的；leaking tap 漏水的水龙头；public transport 公共交通；recycle 回收利用。

## 完形填空 58 · 科技发展（Technology and Our Life）

**Directions:** 阅读下面短文，从短文后各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Technology has changed our lives greatly. In the past, people could only write letters to __1__, but now we can make video calls with anyone in the world. Technology has made our life much more convenient.

One of the greatest inventions is the Internet. It __2__ people and information all over the world. With the Internet, we can learn knowledge, do shopping and enjoy entertainment without leaving home. It has become an important part of our daily life.

Another amazing technology is artificial intelligence, or AI for short. AI can help us do many things. It can answer questions, translate languages and even drive cars. In hospitals, AI helps doctors find diseases more __3__. In factories, AI-controlled robots do dangerous or repeated work. AI is making our work and life __4__.

However, technology also brings some problems. First, some people spend too much time on their phones and computers, which is bad for their __5__. Second, personal information may be misused on the Internet. Third, as machines become smarter, some workers may lose their __6__. Fourth, some young people become too dependent on technology and lose the ability to think or solve problems by themselves.

So we should use technology in a __7__ way. On the one hand, we should make good use of it to improve our life and work. On the other hand, we should not let it __8__ us. We should control the time we spend on screens, protect our personal information and keep learning new skills.

In a word, technology is a double-edged sword. Whether it brings us good or bad __9__ on how we use it. If we use it wisely, it will serve us and make the world a better __10__.

**参考译文：** 科技极大地改变了我们的生活。过去，人们只能通过写信与他人交流，而现在我们可以和世界上任何人视频通话。科技让我们的生活方便了许多。

最伟大的发明之一是互联网。它连接着全世界的人和资讯。有了互联网，我们可以足不出户地学习知识、购物和享受娱乐。它已成为我们日常生活的重要部分。

另一项令人惊叹的技术是人工智能，简称AI。AI能帮我们做许多事。它能回答问题、翻译语言，甚至驾驶汽车。在医院里，AI帮助医生更准确地发现疾病；在工厂里，AI控制的机器人做危险或重复的工作。AI正让我们的工作和生活更便捷。

然而，科技也带来一些问题。首先，有些人花太多时间在手机和电脑上，这对他们的健康有害。其次，个人信息可能在互联网上被滥用。第三，随着机器变得更聪明，一些工人可能失业。第四，有些年轻人变得过度依赖科技，失去了独立思考和解决问题的能力。

所以我们应该以明智的方式使用科技。一方面，我们应该好好利用它来改善生活和工作；另一方面，我们不应该让它控制我们。我们应该控制看屏幕的时间，保护个人信息，不断学习新技能。

总之，科技是一把双刃剑。它给我们带来的是好是坏，取决于我们如何使用它。如果我们明智地使用，它就会为我们服务，让世界变得更美好。

**1.** A. communicate　B. play　C. work　D. sleep

**2.** A. separates　B. connects　C. divides　D. hides

**3.** A. slowly　B. late　C. hardly　D. quickly

**4.** A. harder　B. slower　C. easier　D. worse

**5.** A. money　B. health　C. wealth　D. time

**6.** A. money　B. friends　C. jobs　D. hobbies

**7.** A. careless　B. lazy　C. wrong　D. proper

**8.** A. help　B. serve　C. control　D. save

**9.** A. calls　B. depends　C. works　D. puts

**10.** A. problem　B. trouble　C. danger　D. place

**参考答案：** 1. A　2. B　3. D　4. C　5. B　6. C　7. D　8. C　9. B　10. D

**解析：**
- **1. A** communicate：过去人们只能写信"交流"，与现在可视频通话形成对比。B play、C work、D sleep 均与"写信"的功能不符。
- **2. B** connects：互联网把全世界的人和资讯"连接"起来。A separates、C divides 相反，D hides 不合。
- **3. D** quickly：在医院里，AI 帮助医生更"快"地发现疾病，与 AI 提高效率一致。A slowly、B late、C hardly 均相反。
- **4. C** easier：AI 让我们的工作和生活更"轻松"，与"机器人做危险、重复的工作"呼应。A harder、B slower、D worse 均相反。
- **5. B** health：长时间看手机电脑，对"健康"有害。A money、C wealth、D time 均不能与 be bad for 构成"危害健康"这一常见搭配。
- **6. C** jobs：机器越来越聪明，一些工人可能失去"工作"，与"AI 代替人"呼应。A money、B friends、D hobbies 均不合。
- **7. D** proper：应以"恰当的"方式使用科技，与下文"用得好、不让它控制我们"呼应。A careless、B lazy、C wrong 均负面。
- **8. C** control：不能让它"控制"我们，与"控制屏幕时间"呼应。A help、B serve、D save 均与"被支配"无关。
- **9. B** depends：`depend on` 意为"取决于"，科技带来的好坏取决于我们如何使用它。A calls、C works、D puts 均不能与 on 构成此义。
- **10. D** place：用得好，科技会让世界变得"更好"，`a better place` 意为"更美好的地方"。A problem、B trouble、C danger 均为负面。

**重点词汇：** technology 科技；invention 发明；artificial intelligence (AI) 人工智能；translate 翻译；disease 疾病；be dependent on 依赖；double-edged sword 双刃剑。

我写信是想邀请你参加我们学校的英语演讲比赛，比赛将于下周五，也就是6月15日，在学校礼堂举行。

**1.** A. invite　B. order　C. force　D. allow

**2.** A. taken　B. held　C. put　D. made

**3.** A. take　B. cost　C. last　D. spend

**4.** A. money　B. prize　C. time　D. place

**5.** A. forget　B. hide　C. sell　D. exchange

**6.** A. avoid　B. contact　C. ignore　D. thank

**7.** A. sell　B. break　C. lose　D. reserve

**8.** A. look　B. pick　C. give　D. take

**9.** A. terrible　B. boring　C. wonderful　D. sad

**10.** A. money　B. trouble　C. help　D. reply

**参考答案：** 1. A　2. B　3. C　4. B　5. D　6. B　7. D　8. B　9. C　10. D

**解析：**
- **1. A** invite：`invite sb. to do sth.` 固定搭配"邀请某人做某事"，与"邀请函"体裁一致。B order、C force 语气过强，D allow 不合。
- **2. B** held：活动"举行"用 `be held`，与"演讲比赛将在学校礼堂举行"呼应。A taken、C put、D made 均不能与活动搭配表"举行"。
- **3. C** last：`last about two hours` 意为"持续约两小时"，与 start 呼应，说明时长。A take、B cost、D spend 均不能以 the contest 作主语表"持续"。
- **4. B** prize：`win the first prize` 固定搭配"获得一等奖"，与"你在比赛中"呼应。A money、C time、D place 均不能与 the first 构成"一等奖"。
- **5. D** exchange：`exchange ideas with sb.` 意为"与某人交流想法"，与"遇到英语老师"呼应。A forget、B hide、C sell 均不合。
- **6. B** contact：请你在 6 月 12 日前"联系"我，`contact sb.` 符合。A avoid、C ignore、D thank 均与"回复确认"不符。
- **7. D** reserve：`reserve a seat` 意为"预留一个座位"，与"方便安排"呼应。A sell、B break、C lose 均不合。
- **8. B** pick：`pick sb. up` 意为"开车接某人"，与"在地铁站接你"呼应。A look、C give、D take 均不能与 up 构成"接人"。
- **9. C** wonderful：我相信我们会一起度过"美好"时光，是邀请信常用的友好表达。A terrible、B boring、D sad 均为负面。
- **10. D** reply：`Looking forward to your reply.` 是书信结尾的固定客套话"期待你的回复"。A money、B trouble、C help 均不合。

**重点词汇：** invite 邀请；attend 参加；be held 举行；last 持续；audience 观众；exchange 交流；reserve 预订；pick sb. up 接某人；look forward to 期待。

**1.** A. organize　B. cancel　C. stop　D. close

**2.** A. put　B. held　C. made　D. taken

**3.** A. afraid　B. shy　C. brave　D. silent

**4.** A. problem　B. trouble　C. danger　D. chance

**5.** A. sign　B. put　C. take　D. give

**解析：**

- **3. C** brave：在英语角要"勇敢"开口说，与"别担心说不好"呼应。A afraid、B shy、D silent 均与"speak out"矛盾。
- **4. D** chance：英语歌唱比赛是你展示才华的好"机会"，`a good chance` 符合。A problem、B trouble、C danger 均为负面。
- **5. A** sign：`sign up` 固定短语"报名"，与"5 月 15 日前在学生处报名"呼应。B put up、C take up、D give up 均无"报名"义。
- **6. B** tips：史密斯老师会与我们分享一些有用的"建议"，与"如何学好英语"呼应。A money、C food、D clothes 均不合。
- **7. C** rules：参加活动要注意以下"规定/规则"，与后文 First/Second/Third 的条目呼应。A money、B friends、D games 均不合。
- **8. D** late：请准时到场、不要"迟到"，与 arrive on time 相对。A early 相反，B again、C away 不合。
- **9. B** website：可以打电话或访问我们的"网站"了解更多信息。A money、C friends、D teachers 均不能与 visit 搭配表"访问网站"。
- **10. C** use：希望大家享受活动，"好好利用"课余时间，`make good use of` 固定搭配。A waste、B trouble、D mistake 均不合。

**重点词汇：** notice 通知；a series of 一系列；English Corner 英语角；spoken English 英语口语；lecture 讲座；sign up 报名；Students' Union 学生会；make good use of 好好利用。

## 完形填空 61 · 应用文（A Diary：A Meaningful Day）

**Directions:** 阅读下面的日记，从各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

**May 10th　Friday　Sunny**

Today was a very meaningful day for me. In the morning, our class went to a nursing home to __1__ the old people there.

When we arrived, we helped them clean the rooms and wash their clothes. Then we sat down and talked with them. Some old people told us interesting stories about the __2__, and we listened carefully. One old lady held my hand and said she was very __3__ to see us.

At noon, we put on a small __4__ for them. We sang songs, danced and told jokes. When we saw the __5__ on their faces, we felt very happy too.

In the afternoon, we gave them the gifts we had made by __6__. They were simple, but the old people liked them very much. An old man said to me, "Thank you, children. You made our day __7__."

On the way back to school, I thought a lot. The old people need not only food and clothes but also __8__ and love. They may feel lonely because their children are busy and __9__ come to see them. A short visit can bring them great happiness.

Today I learned that helping others is also helping ourselves. When we bring joy to others, we feel happy too. I decided to come here again and do more for those in __10__.

What a meaningful day it was!

**参考译文：** 5月10日　星期五　晴

今天对我来说是非常有意义的一天。上午，我们班去了一家养老院看望那里的老人。

我们到达后，帮他们打扫房间、洗衣服。然后我们坐下来和他们聊天。一些老人给我们讲了关于过去的有趣故事，我们认真地听着。一位老奶奶握着我的手，说她见到我们非常高兴。

中午，我们为他们表演了一个小节目。我们唱歌、跳舞、讲笑话。当我们看到他们脸上的笑容时，我们也感到非常开心。

下午，我们把亲手做的礼物送给他们。礼物虽然简单，但老人们非常喜欢。一位老人对我说，谢谢你们，孩子们，你们让我们这一天过得很美好。

在回学校的路上，我想了很多。老人们不仅需要食物和衣服，还需要关心和爱。他们可能会感到孤独，因为他们的子女很忙，很少来看他们。一次短短的探望就能给他们带来巨大的快乐。

今天我明白了，帮助他人也是在帮助自己。当我们给他人带来快乐时，自己也感到快乐。我决定以后还要再来这里，为那些有需要的人多做些事。

多么有意义的一天啊！

**1.** A. visit　B. hit　C. leave　D. forget

**2.** A. future　B. past　C. money　D. trouble

**3.** A. sad　B. angry　C. happy　D. tired

**4.** A. mistake　B. mess　C. noise　D. show

**5.** A. tears　B. smiles　C. anger　D. fear

**6.** A. themselves　B. himself　C. herself　D. ourselves

**7.** A. bright　B. dark　C. sad　D. cold

**8.** A. money　B. food　C. care　D. clothes

**9.** A. often　B. seldom　C. always　D. usually

**10.** A. joy　B. school　C. need　D. work

**参考答案：** 1. A　2. B　3. C　4. D　5. B　6. D　7. A　8. C　9. B　10. C

**解析：**
- **1. A** visit：我们去养老院"看望"老人，`visit` 符合志愿活动语境。B hit、C leave、D forget 均不合。
- **2. B** past：老人给我们讲"过去"的故事，与"我们认真听"呼应。A future 相反，C money、D trouble 均与"故事"无关。
- **3. C** happy：老太太说见到我们很"高兴"，与后文"我们也很开心"呼应。A sad、B angry、D tired 均相反。
- **4. D** show：中午我们为他们表演了一个小"节目"，`put on a show` 固定搭配。A mistake、B mess、C noise 均不合。
- **5. B** smiles：看到他们脸上的"笑容"，我们也很开心。A tears、C anger、D fear 均与"开心"矛盾。
- **6. D** ourselves：礼物是我们"自己"亲手做的，`by ourselves` 与主语 we 一致。A themselves、B himself、C herself 均不一致。
- **7. A** bright：老人说"你们让我们的日子明亮起来"，`make one's day bright` 与"带来快乐"呼应。B dark、C sad、D cold 均相反。
- **8. C** care：老人不仅需要衣食，还需要"关怀"和爱，与"我们去看望他们"呼应。A money、B food、D clothes 都与 not only...but also 后半部分重复或不合。
- **9. B** seldom：子女很忙，所以"很少"来看他们，解释了老人的孤独。A often、C always、D usually 均与"孤独"矛盾。
- **10. C** need：我决定再来，为那些"需要帮助"的人做更多事，`those in need` 固定短语。A joy、B school、D work 均不能构成此义。

**重点词汇：** meaningful 有意义的；nursing home 养老院；put on a show 表演节目；by oneself 亲自，独自；lonely 孤独的；in need 需要帮助的。

## 完形填空 62 · 应用文（A Speech：Cherish Your School Life）

**Directions:** 阅读下面的演讲稿，从各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Good morning, everyone! It is my great __1__ to speak here today. My topic is "Cherish Your School Life".

School life is one of the most beautiful __2__ in our life. We spend many years studying here, and during this time we grow from children into young adults. We make friends, learn knowledge and develop our character. These years will become the most valuable __3__ in the future.

However, many students don't realize this. They complain about too much homework, boring classes and strict teachers. They may __4__ their time playing games or sleeping in class. When they look back one day, they may feel sorry for what they have done.

So how should we cherish our school life? First, study hard. Knowledge is the best __5__ we can give ourselves. The more we learn now, the better we can deal with the __6__ in the future. Second, make good friends. Your classmates will be your lifelong friends. Study together, play together and grow together. Third, take part in activities. Joining clubs, contests and sports can make our school life more colorful and help us learn skills that we cannot learn from books. Fourth, respect your teachers. They work hard to help us and care about us. We should be __7__ for their efforts.

Of course, school life is not always easy. We may __8__ exams, quarrel with friends or feel stressed. But these difficulties are also part of our growth. They teach us how to face problems and become __9__.

In a word, school life is short and __10__. Let's cherish every day, work hard and enjoy it. When we leave school one day, we will be proud of what we have done here. Thank you for listening!

**参考译文：** 大家早上好！今天能在这里讲话，我深感荣幸。我的主题是“珍惜你的校园生活”。

校园生活是我们一生中最美好的时光之一。我们在这里学习多年，在这段时间里，我们从孩子成长为青年。我们结交朋友、学习知识、塑造品格。这些年将来会成为我们最宝贵的回忆。

然而，许多学生没有意识到这一点。他们抱怨作业太多、课程无聊、老师太严。他们可能把时间浪费在玩游戏或上课睡觉上。当他们有一天回首往事时，可能会为自己的所作所为感到后悔。

那么，我们应该怎样珍惜校园生活呢？首先，努力学习。知识是我们能给自己最好的礼物。现在学得越多，将来就越能应对挑战。其次，结交好朋友。你的同学将成为你终生的朋友，一起学习、一起玩耍、一起成长。第三，参加活动。加入社团、比赛和体育运动，能让校园生活更丰富多彩，还能帮我们学到书本上学不到的技能。第四，尊敬老师。他们努力工作来帮助我们、关心我们，我们应该感谢他们的付出。

当然，校园生活并不总是轻松。我们可能会考试失利、和朋友吵架或感到压力。但这些困难也是我们成长的一部分，它们教会我们如何面对问题，变得坚强。

总之，校园生活短暂而宝贵。让我们珍惜每一天，努力学习，尽情享受。当我们有一天离开学校时，会为自己在这里所做的一切感到自豪。谢谢大家的聆听！

**1.** A. honor　B. trouble　C. fear　D. problem

**2.** A. periods　B. problems　C. troubles　D. dangers

**3.** A. money　B. friends　C. memories　D. jobs

**4.** A. save　B. use　C. value　D. waste

**5.** A. money　B. gift　C. friend　D. job

**6.** A. money　B. friends　C. challenges　D. games

**7.** A. sorry　B. thankful　C. angry　D. sad

**8.** A. pass　B. fail　C. take　D. like

**9.** A. weaker　B. sadder　C. stronger　D. lazier

**10.** A. long　B. boring　C. sad　D. precious

**参考答案：** 1. A　2. A　3. C　4. D　5. B　6. C　7. B　8. B　9. C　10. D

**解析：**
- **1. A** honor：`It is my great honor to...` 是演讲开场白"我很荣幸……"。B trouble、C fear、D problem 均为负面。
- **2. A** periods：学校生活是我们人生中最美好的"时期"之一，`period` 意为"时期"。B problems、C troubles、D dangers 均为负面。
- **3. C** memories：这些年会成为将来最珍贵的"回忆"，与"多年后回首"呼应。A money、B friends、D jobs 均不合。
- **4. D** waste：他们可能把时间"浪费"在打游戏或上课睡觉上。A save、B use、C value 均与"将来后悔"矛盾。
- **5. B** gift：知识是我们能给自己最好的"礼物"，与"努力学习"呼应。A money、C friend、D job 均不符。
- **6. C** challenges：现在学得越多，将来越能应对"挑战"，`deal with challenges` 固定搭配。A money、B friends、D games 均不合。
- **7. B** thankful：老师辛勤付出，我们应"心存感激"，`be thankful for` 固定搭配。A sorry、C angry、D sad 均不合。
- **8. B** fail：我们可能考试"不及格"、与朋友争吵或感到压力，与"困难也是成长的一部分"呼应。A pass、C take、D like 均不是困难。
- **9. C** stronger：这些困难教会我们面对问题、变得更"坚强"。A weaker、B sadder、D lazier 均相反。
- **10. D** precious：学校生活短暂而"宝贵"，与"珍惜每一天"呼应。A long 相反，B boring、C sad 均为负面。

**重点词汇：** cherish 珍惜；honor 荣幸；character 品格；complain about 抱怨；lifelong 终身的；be thankful for 感激；precious 宝贵的；be proud of 为……自豪。

## 完形填空 63 · 应用文（A Letter of Application）

**Directions:** 阅读下面的求职信，从各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Dear Sir or Madam,

I am writing to __1__ for the position of sales assistant in your company. I saw your advertisement in the newspaper yesterday and I am very __2__ in this job.

I am a 22-year-old graduate from a college. During my school years, I studied business management and got good __3__ in most subjects. I have also learned how to use the computer and how to speak English. I believe these skills will help me do the __4__ well.

In my spare time, I like reading and playing basketball. I am a __5__ person and I get along well with others. I am not afraid of __6__ and I am willing to work hard.

I would like to have an __7__ so that I can tell you more about myself. I can come at any time that suits you. I would be __8__ if you could give me a chance. Please __9__ me at 138-0000-0000 or by email.

Thank you for your time. I am looking __10__ to hearing from you.

Yours sincerely,
Li Hua

**参考译文：** 尊敬的先生或女士：

我写信是为了申请贵公司销售助理一职。昨天我在报纸上看到了你们的招聘广告，我对这份工作非常感兴趣。

我是一名22岁的大学毕业生。在校期间，我学习商务管理，大多数科目都取得了好成绩。我还学会了使用电脑和说英语。我相信这些技能会帮助我做好这份工作。

业余时间，我喜欢阅读和打篮球。我是一个友善的人，和别人相处得很好。我不怕困难，也愿意努力工作。

我希望有一次面试机会，这样我就能更多地介绍自己。我可以在你们方便的任何时间前来。如果您能给我一个机会，我将非常感激。请拨打138-0000-0000或通过电子邮件联系我。

感谢您抽出时间。期待您的回复。

你真诚的，李华

**1.** A. apply　B. ask　C. look　D. wait

**2.** A. weak　B. interested　C. bored　D. rich

**3.** A. results　B. news　C. reasons　D. gifts

**4.** A. job　B. housework　C. shopping　D. homework

**5.** A. lazy　B. friendly　C. shy　D. careless

**6.** A. success　B. money　C. difficulties　D. happiness

**7.** A. interview　B. exam　C. answer　D. excuse

**8.** A. sorry　B. thankful　C. angry　D. sad

**9.** A. meet　B. see　C. call　D. visit

**10.** A. up　B. down　C. forward　D. back

**参考答案：** 1. A　2. B　3. A　4. A　5. B　6. C　7. A　8. B　9. C　10. C

**解析：**
- **1. A** apply：`apply for` 意为"申请（职位）"，与"求职信"主题呼应。B ask、C look、D wait 均不能与 for 构成"申请"之意。
- **2. B** interested：`be interested in` 意为"对……感兴趣"，是求职信常用表达。A weak、C bored、D rich 均不合语境。
- **3. A** results：在学校里"取得好成绩"用 `get good results`。B news、C reasons、D gifts 均不符。
- **4. A** job：这些技能能帮助我做好"工作"，与"申请职位"呼应。B housework、C shopping、D homework 均与职场无关。
- **5. B** friendly：简历中形容自己"友善、好相处"，与 `get along well with others` 呼应。A lazy、C shy、D careless 均为负面。
- **6. C** difficulties：`be afraid of difficulties` 意为"不怕困难"，是求职常用语。A success、B money、D happiness 均不需"怕"。
- **7. A** interview：求职信结尾常写 `have an interview`（参加面试）。B exam、C answer、D excuse 均不合。
- **8. B** thankful：`I would be thankful if...` 表示"如果您能……我将非常感激"。A sorry、C angry、D sad 均不合。
- **9. C** call：后面跟着电话号码，说明是"打电话"联系，`call sb. at...` 固定搭配。A meet、B see、D visit 均不接电话号码。
- **10. C** forward：`look forward to doing` 意为"期待做某事"，是书信常用结尾。A up、B down、D back 均不能构成此短语。

**重点词汇：** apply for 申请；position 职位；advertisement 广告；graduate 毕业生；business management 商务管理；get along well with 与……相处融洽；be afraid of 害怕；interview 面试；look forward to 期待。

## 完形填空 64 · 记叙文（A Special Gift）

**Directions:** 阅读下面短文，从各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Yesterday was my mother's birthday. I wanted to give her a special __1__. But I didn't have much money, so I decided to make a gift by __2__.

In the morning, I got up early and went to the kitchen. I tried to make a cake for her. It was my __3__ time to cook, so I was very nervous. I followed the instructions carefully, but I still made a few __4__. When the cake came out of the oven, its shape was strange, but it __5__ good.

In the afternoon, I wrote a card. On it I wrote, "Thank you for your love. You are the best mother in the world." Then I cleaned the house and put some flowers on the table.

When my mother came home in the evening, she was __6__ to see the cake and the card. Her eyes were full of tears. She said it was the best gift she had ever __7__. We sat together, ate the cake and talked happily.

That day I learned an important lesson: a gift is not __8__ by its price, but by the love in it. A small gift made with heart can make people much __9__ than an expensive one. This is the true __10__ of giving.

**参考译文：** 昨天是我妈妈的生日。我想送她一份特别的惊喜。但我没有多少钱，所以我决定自己动手做一份礼物。

早上，我早早起床走进厨房，试着为她做一个蛋糕。这是我第一次做饭，所以我非常紧张。我仔细按照说明操作，但还是犯了一些错。当蛋糕从烤箱里出来时，形状很奇怪，但尝起来味道不错。

下午，我写了一张卡片。上面写着，谢谢您的爱，您是世上最好的妈妈。然后我打扫了房子，并在桌上摆了一些花。

晚上妈妈回家时，看到蛋糕和卡片，感到很惊讶。她眼里满是泪水。她说这是她收到过的最好的礼物。我们坐在一起，吃着蛋糕，愉快地聊天。

那一天我学到了重要的一课：礼物不是用价格来评判的，而是用其中的爱来衡量的。用心做的小礼物，比昂贵的礼物更能让人快乐。这才是给予真正的意义。

**1.** A. problem　B. surprise　C. question　D. mistake

**2.** A. yourself　B. himself　C. myself　D. herself

**3.** A. last　B. free　C. busy　D. first

**4.** A. mistakes　B. friends　C. plans　D. cakes

**5.** A. looked　B. sounded　C. tasted　D. felt

**6.** A. surprised　B. sad　C. angry　D. worried

**7.** A. bought　B. received　C. made　D. found

**8.** A. decided　B. measured　C. judged　D. chosen

**9.** A. sadder　B. busier　C. poorer　D. happier

**10.** A. price　B. color　C. meaning　D. size

**参考答案：** 1. B　2. C　3. D　4. A　5. C　6. A　7. B　8. C　9. D　10. C

**解析：**
- **1. B** surprise：给妈妈生日"惊喜"，`give sb. a surprise` 是常用搭配。A problem、C question、D mistake 均为负面词。
- **2. C** myself：主语是 I，所以用反身代词 myself，"自己动手做礼物"。A yourself、B himself、D herself 人称都不对。
- **3. D** first：这是我"第一次"做饭，所以很紧张。A last、B free、C busy 都不符合"紧张"的原因。
- **4. A** mistakes：虽然照着说明做，还是"犯了一些错"。B friends、C plans、D cakes 与"犯错"无关。
- **5. C** tasted：蛋糕形状奇怪，但"尝起来"不错，食物用 taste 表示味道。A looked、B sounded、D felt 均不能与 good 表示味道。
- **6. A** surprised：妈妈回家看到蛋糕和卡片感到"惊讶"。B sad、C angry、D worried 均与"感动落泪"矛盾。
- **7. B** received：妈妈说这是她"收到过"的最好的礼物，`the best gift she had ever received` 语意通顺。A bought、C made、D found 均不合。
- **8. C** judged：礼物不应"以价格来评判"，`be judged by` 意为"以……来评判"。A decided、B measured、D chosen 均不符。
- **9. D** happier：用心做的小礼物能让人比昂贵礼物更"快乐"。A sadder、B busier、C poorer 均不合逻辑。
- **10. C** meaning：这才是给予真正的"意义"，与全文主旨呼应。A price、B color、D size 均太具体。

**重点词汇：** surprise 惊喜；by myself 我自己；nervous 紧张的；instruction 说明；oven 烤箱；be full of 充满；tear 眼泪；judge 评判；expensive 昂贵的。

## 完形填空 65 · 说明文（How to Improve Your Memory）

**Directions:** 阅读下面短文，从各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Everyone wants to have a good memory. A good memory can help us study and work better. But how can we __1__ our memory? Here are some useful tips.

First, get enough sleep. When we sleep, our brain __2__ the things we learned during the day. If we don't sleep well, we may forget things __3__. So try to sleep for eight hours every night.

Second, eat healthy food. Fish, eggs, nuts and vegetables are good __4__ our brain. They can make our memory stronger. On the other hand, too much junk food is bad for it.

Third, exercise often. Doing sports can make more blood go to the brain, and this helps us think more __5__. Even a short walk every day is helpful.

Fourth, repeat and review. If you want to remember something, don't just read it once. Read it again and again, and try to use it in your own words. The more you __6__ it, the longer you will keep it in mind.

Fifth, stay relaxed. Too much worry or fear can make it hard to remember. When you feel __7__, take a deep breath and calm down.

Finally, link new things with old ones. For example, if you want to remember a new word, you can connect it with a story or a picture. This makes the memory more __8__ and easier to recall.

In a word, a good memory is not only a gift but also a __9__. If we form good habits and keep __10__ them, we can all have a better memory.

**参考译文：** 每个人都想拥有好记性。好记性能帮助我们更好地学习和工作。但我们怎样才能提高记忆力呢？这里有一些有用的建议。

首先，保证充足的睡眠。当我们睡觉时，大脑会整理白天学到的东西。如果我们睡不好，就可能很容易忘事。所以尽量每晚睡足八小时。

其次，吃健康的食物。鱼、鸡蛋、坚果和蔬菜对大脑有益，它们能让我们的记忆力更强。另一方面，太多垃圾食品则对它有害。

第三，经常锻炼。做运动能让更多血液流向大脑，这有助于我们更清晰地思考。即使每天散散步也很有帮助。

第四，重复和复习。如果你想记住某件事，不要只读一遍。反复地读，并试着用自己的话把它说出来。你复习得越多，就能记得越久。

第五，保持放松。过多的担忧或恐惧会让人难以记忆。当你感到紧张时，深呼吸，冷静下来。

最后，把新事物和旧事物联系起来。例如，如果你想记住一个新单词，可以把它和一个故事或一幅图画联系起来。这会让记忆更清晰，也更容易回想起来。

总之，好记性不仅是一种天赋，也是一种技能。如果我们养成良好的习惯并坚持练习，就都能拥有更好的记忆力。

**1.** A. lose　B. improve　C. forget　D. break

**2.** A. throws　B. sells　C. organizes　D. loses

**3.** A. easily　B. hardly　C. never　D. seldom

**4.** A. at　B. on　C. with　D. for

**5.** A. slowly　B. sadly　C. angrily　D. clearly

**6.** A. review　B. throw　C. hide　D. waste

**7.** A. nervous　B. happy　C. excited　D. proud

**8.** A. boring　B. difficult　C. clear　D. dark

**9.** A. problem　B. skill　C. danger　D. trouble

**10.** A. breaking　B. forgetting　C. practicing　D. hiding

**参考答案：** 1. B　2. C　3. A　4. D　5. D　6. A　7. A　8. C　9. B　10. C

**解析：**
- **1. B** improve：全文讲"如何提高记忆力"，`improve one's memory` 意为"改善记忆力"。A lose、C forget、D break 均与"提高"矛盾。
- **2. C** organizes：睡觉时大脑会"整理"白天学到的东西，符合科学常识。A throws、B sells、D loses 均不合。
- **3. A** easily：睡不好就"容易"忘事，与上文"睡眠很重要"呼应。B hardly、C never、D seldom 均表示"不"，与逻辑矛盾。
- **4. D** for：`be good for` 意为"对……有益"，指食物对大脑有好处。A at、B on、C with 均不能构成此意。
- **5. D** clearly：运动让血液流向大脑，有助于"清晰地"思考。A slowly、B sadly、C angrily 均不合语境。
- **6. A** review：想要记住就要不断"复习"，`The more you review it, the longer you will keep it in mind`。B throw、C hide、D waste 均相反。
- **7. A** nervous：遇到"紧张"情绪时要深呼吸、冷静下来，与 `stay relaxed` 呼应。B happy、C excited、D proud 均不需要"冷静"。
- **8. C** clear：把新事物和故事、图片联系起来，会让记忆更"清晰"。A boring、B difficult、D dark 均与"更容易回忆"矛盾。
- **9. B** skill：好记忆不仅是天赋，也是一种"技能"，需要通过练习获得。A problem、C danger、D trouble 均为负面词。
- **10. C** practicing：养成好习惯并坚持"练习"，才能拥有好记忆力。A breaking、B forgetting、D hiding 均不合。

**重点词汇：** memory 记忆力；organize 整理；junk food 垃圾食品；blood 血液；review 复习；recall 回忆；relaxed 放松的；form a habit 养成习惯；not only...but also 不仅……而且。

## 完形填空 66 · 议论文（Teamwork in Business）

**Directions:** 阅读下面短文，从各题所给的 A、B、C、D 四个选项中，选出可以填入空白处的最佳选项。

Nowadays, more and more companies pay attention to teamwork. They believe that a group of people working together can __1__ more than one person working alone. In fact, teamwork plays an important __2__ in the success of a business.

First, teamwork can bring different ideas together. Every member has his or her own __3__ and experience. When they share these with each other, the team can find better ways to __4__ problems. A difficult task that seems impossible for one person may become __5__ when the whole team works on it.

Second, teamwork can save time and energy. If members __6__ their work properly, everyone will do what he or she is good at. In this way, the work can be finished more __7__ and with fewer mistakes.

Third, teamwork can make people feel happy. Working alone may make one feel __8__, while working in a team gives people a sense of belonging. They support each other, learn from each other and grow together. This is good for both the workers and the company.

Of course, teamwork is not always easy. Members may have different opinions and even quarrel sometimes. So good __9__ is very important. A good leader should listen to everyone and help them work toward the same goal.

In short, teamwork is the key to success in modern business. As the saying goes, "Two heads are better than one." If we learn to work together, we can achieve things that we could never do __10__.

**参考译文：** 如今，越来越多的公司重视团队合作。他们相信一群人一起工作能比一个人单干取得更多成果。事实上，团队合作在企业成功中起着重要作用。

首先，团队合作能把不同的想法汇聚在一起。每个成员都有自己的想法和经验。当他们彼此分享时，团队就能找到更好的方法来解决问题。一项对一个人来说似乎不可能完成的艰巨任务，当整个团队一起做时，可能就会变得容易。

其次，团队合作能节省时间和精力。如果成员恰当分工，每个人都会做自己擅长的事。这样，工作能更快完成，错误也更少。

第三，团队合作能让人感到快乐。一个人工作可能让人感到孤独，而在团队中工作会给人一种归属感。他们互相支持、互相学习、共同成长。这对员工和公司都有好处。

当然，团队合作并不总是容易的。成员可能有不同的意见，有时甚至会争吵。所以良好的领导力非常重要。一个好的领导者应该倾听每个人的意见，帮助他们朝着同一个目标努力。

总之，团队合作是现代企业成功的关键。俗话说，三个臭皮匠，顶个诸葛亮。如果我们学会合作，就能完成一个人独自做不到的事情。

**1.** A. achieve　B. lose　C. hide　D. waste

**2.** A. game　B. problem　C. role　D. mistake

**3.** A. money　B. clothes　C. houses　D. ideas

**4.** A. make　B. create　C. solve　D. find

**5.** A. harder　B. easier　C. slower　D. worse

**6.** A. hide　B. refuse　C. forget　D. share

**7.** A. quickly　B. slowly　C. badly　D. sadly

**8.** A. happy　B. lonely　C. proud　D. safe

**9.** A. food　B. money　C. leadership　D. luck

**10.** A. together　B. quickly　C. well　D. alone

**参考答案：** 1. A　2. C　3. D　4. C　5. B　6. D　7. A　8. B　9. C　10. D

**解析：**
- **1. A** achieve：团队合作能"取得"比一个人单干更多的成果，与下文"成功"呼应。B lose、C hide、D waste 均为负面词。
- **2. C** role：`play an important role in` 意为"在……中起重要作用"，是固定搭配。A game、B problem、D mistake 均不能构成此短语。
- **3. D** ideas：每个成员都有自己的"想法"和经验，与下文 `share these` 及 `different ideas` 呼应。A money、B clothes、C houses 均不合。
- **4. C** solve：找到更好的办法来"解决问题"，`solve problems` 是固定搭配。A make、B create、D find 均不能与 problems 搭配成"解决"之意。
- **5. B** easier：一个人觉得不可能的任务，全队一起做就会变得"更容易"。A harder、C slower、D worse 均与逻辑相反。
- **6. D** share：如果成员恰当"分工/分享"工作，各人做自己擅长的事，与上文 share 呼应。A hide、B refuse、C forget 均不合理。
- **7. A** quickly：工作能更"快"完成，而且错误更少，与 `save time` 呼应。B slowly、C badly、D sadly 均不合。
- **8. B** lonely：一个人工作会让人感到"孤独"，与团队带来的归属感形成对比。A happy、C proud、D safe 均与对比不符。
- **9. C** leadership：成员意见不合时，良好的"领导"就很重要，下文 `A good leader` 是提示。A food、B money、D luck 均不符。
- **10. D** alone：学会合作，就能做到一个人"独自"做不到的事，与全文"团队合作"主题呼应。A together、B quickly、C well 均不合。

**重点词汇：** teamwork 团队合作；pay attention to 重视；achieve 取得；play a role in 在……中起作用；solve problems 解决问题；belonging 归属感；support 支持；quarrel 争吵；leadership 领导力；Two heads are better than one 三个臭皮匠顶个诸葛亮。

# 附录（Appendix）

## 附录一　完形填空解题技巧

| 步骤 | 做法 | 要点提示 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 第一步　通读全文 | 跳过空格，先快速读一遍短文 | 抓住文章中心和作者的写作思路，切忌见空就填 |
| 第二步　逐空试填 | 结合上下文，先填有把握的空 | 简单题先做，为难题提供线索 |
| 第三步　逻辑推断 | 分析前后句的因果、转折、并列关系 | 关注 but / so / because / however / on the other hand 等信号词 |
| 第四步　复查验证 | 把所选词代入原文再读一遍 | 检查词义、语法、搭配、时态、语序是否通顺 | 

**答题三大原则：**
1. **先懂大意，再选答案**——完形是"语篇题"，答案往往藏在上下文里。
2. **先易后难，边做边等**——难题的线索常常在后面的句子中。
3. **词义优先，语法把关**——先看意思合不合，再看搭配对不对。

---

## 附录二　常考"上下文线索词"速查

| 逻辑关系 | 常见信号词 | 提醒 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 因果 | because, so, since, as a result, thanks to | 看到原因找结果，看到结果找原因 |
| 转折 | but, however, yet, while, on the other hand | 前后意思相反，往往是解题关键 |
| 并列 | and, also, as well as, besides, moreover | 前后意思相近，可推测同义词 |
| 递进 | even, more importantly, what's more | 程度加深，注意比较级 |
| 举例 | for example, such as, like, for instance | 例子是对前文的解释 |
| 强调 | in fact, indeed, of course | 后面常是作者真实观点 |

---

## 附录三　高频固定搭配速查（按字母排序）

| 搭配 | 含义 | 搭配 | 含义 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| apply for | 申请 | be proud of | 为……自豪 |

## 附录四　常见易错点提醒

1. **转折语境选反义词**
   - 看到 but / however，前后意思相反。如"形状奇怪，__ 尝起来不错"，应选 tasted（好的一面）。

2. **因果语境找对应**
   - 前半句说"睡不好"，后半句应选"容易忘事（easily forget）"，而不是 hardly / never。

3. **否定句用 either，不用 too/also**
   - 肯定句用 too / also；否定句用 either。如 `He doesn't like it, either.`

4. **反身代词与人称一致**
   - by myself / yourself / himself / herself 必须与主语一致。

5. **固定搭配整体记忆**
   - look forward to **doing**、be used to **doing**、be good at **doing**，介词后接动词 -ing 形式。

6. **注意比较级结构**
   - `The more..., the more...`（越……越……）；`more...than`（比……更……）。

7. **形容词的"感官"用法**
   - look（看起来）、sound（听起来）、taste（尝起来）、feel（感觉）后接形容词，不用副词。

8. **主旨类空格回到首尾找**
   - 文章首句和末句常体现中心和态度，主旨题（如 role / meaning / key）优先在此寻找线索。

9. **同义线索词**
   - 上下文中出现近义或同义表达时，往往提示答案。如 `support each other` 提示 `belonging`。

10. **先跳过生词，别被吓住**
    - 生词不影响做题，抓住句子主干和逻辑关系即可选出答案。

## 附录五　66 篇题材分布一览

| 题材类别 | 篇号 | 篇数 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 记叙文（故事、家庭、校园、成长） | 1–16、64 | 17 |
| 说明文（科普、文化、生活常识） | 17–32、56、57、58、65 | 20 |
| 议论文／社会话题 | 33–46、66 | 15 |
| 商务职场／经济 | 47–54 | 8 |
| 健康／环境／科技 | 55–58 | 4 |
| 应用文（书信、通知、日记、演讲等） | 59–63 | 5 |

> 建议每天精做 2 篇，做完后对照解析逐空复盘，把不认识的搭配记入自己的词汇本，考前一周集中回看易错点。
